## 2026-11-03 live-capture safety and prospective-proof checkpoint (partial, not completed)

- **P01-05 / P01-07:** The independent provider has documented credit-based billing (10 credits per 1000-row compact page; Free plan 100–500/month depending on account). Retired the unsafe twice-daily complete-board cron; limited the ordinary full-board schedule to once monthly until an account-specific allowance can be reconciled. Added zero-credit /coverage quota preflight, a 60-credit maximum per run, a 20-credit confirmed workspace reserve, and fail-closed pagination on missing/low credits. A request-based legacy workspace uses its explicit request allowance instead. No automatic overage requests are authorized.
- **NHLQ-03:** The immutable official 2026–27 season schedule now acts as a fallback when the live NHL schedule endpoint is unavailable, while exact time/team identity from live NHL takes precedence. [Passing research test suite](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41750266763).
- **NHLQ-04:** A separately authorized [one-shot trial](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41750336085) returned HTTP 503 from the zero-credit /coverage check and **did not call the billed projections endpoint**. The [second trial](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41750531832) passed the zero-credit preflight and fetched the compact board but found zero qualifying fresh pregame rows at approximately 4:57pm Pacific; prior archived snapshots were retained and **no precommitted selections or hit-rate proof were created**. The provider may have had no eligible future-game offers in that capture window, or a compact-schema mismatch; an aggregate rejection-reason diagnostic now distinguishes these next time. No raw provider response or key was published.
- **Follow-up:** Guarded morning pregame attempts are scheduled for **Nov 4–6, 2026 at 09:25 PST**; a successful Git-archived full compact board disables subsequent one-shot attempts. No newer prospective hit rate or commercially validated betting edge is claimed until selected picks are actually precommitted, NHL official final results are available, and the grader accepts the evidence. New one-off requests remain restricted to explicit workflow changes/dispatch.

## 2026-11-03 parallel development checkpoint — no additional paid API requests (partial tasks, not checked off)

- **P06-02 lineup selection safety (partial):** Standard 2–6 pick research optimizer now deduplicates source-offered directions, alternate lines and markets to **one candidate per player before the top-25 search limit**. Added pre-start chronology and in-game exclusions when source timestamps exist, plus a model probability floor above 50%; no computed joint probability or verified EV. Historic simulation labels are not betting recommendations. [Passing entire NHL research builder suite](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41752813890). Larger work remains: validated correlations, payout rules, cross-platform constraints and prospective lineup grading.
- **EDGE-05 temporal-model reliability (partial):** Archived real-line retrospective training has only **66 decided rows from 8 NHL games**, insufficient for the explicit model-selection research gate of 100 decisions / 20 games. The 2026–27 comparison period has **156 decisions across 13 games**. The comparison now **refuses to name a trained challenger winner** when training coverage fails and rejects contradictory source outcomes against official boxscores. The weighted-form production model remains unchanged. [Historical model lab passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41752734379). Retrospective lines are not prospective validated hits.
- **P01-06 operations health (partial):** Existing no-cost daily GitHub status summary now also audits independent full-board archival completeness, 30-minute source age, research/board lineage, last *archived* API credit balance and missing quota headers. This is not a live credit-account query, actual last-run failure detector or full observability solution. [Health workflow passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41752910336).
- **UX-01 NHL website (partial):** The existing mobile MLB-inspired Top Props → Slip Builder → Results → Advanced Research layout is preserved. Added explicit **Fresh pregame offers (default)** versus **Saved historical snapshot** modes, 30-minute provider-source/prop/event eligibility gates, and refresh of age checks on open browser tabs. Expired or incomplete offers no longer appear as current offers by default; historical research remains inspectable with warnings. [Public dashboard validator passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/41753198098) and [live HTTP smoke passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/41753226736). - **Approved MoneyPuck/DFO research reliability (partial):** Decoupled the independent source runs. If one MoneyPuck season file times out, no incomplete or stale MoneyPuck feature set is promoted, but approved Daily Faceoff reporting can still archive. Reject totally empty new source snapshots. [Source workflow passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41753445936), with archived 743 skaters, 74 goalies, 32 team summaries, 5 matched future-game reports / 10 goalie reports / 9 teams' line-combination records. One separate DFO team page was rejected for staleness/clock anomaly. These are user-reported approved research sources, **not documented commercial licenses or prospective proof**.
- **Overall gate unchanged:** No legitimate explicit frozen standalone prop decisions have yet been graded; do not announce a verified selection hit rate, calibrated probabilities, positive betting EV, or model promotion. No subscription launch is authorized by these partial steps. New NHL API live captures remain budget-guarded; historical MLB repository was not modified.

## Public model privacy and customer-focused website — November 3, 2026 (partial, UX/BIZ/OPS)

- **Public website:** Removed the "Everything behind the model" section and model laboratory explanations from [NHL Research Desk](https://thedarkwillow.github.io/nhl-betting-platform1/). Present only top pregame props, offer-side research, 2–6 pick lineups, line comparisons and honestly labelled prospective outcomes. Keep freshness and original fantasy platform availability/payout disclaimers.
- **Private intellectual property:** The private NHL backend now sanitizes data *at publication time* with `src/nhl-public-dashboard-payload.cjs`. The public `data.json` is an explicit, tested allowlist, excluding formulas, trained/challenger comparison metrics, probability distributions, MoneyPuck features, Daily Faceoff research diagnostics, role/matchup scout internals, retrospective model-lab reports and raw source rows. No paid API key or private backend code is published. The 2–6 pick research output retains only essential customer-facing selections and non-verified performance indicators.
- **Automated checks:** [Public validator passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/41755573329) and [deployed HTTPS smoke passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/41755619323). New backend unit tests reject disclosure of private model fields; CI and publication workflow now trigger when the sanitizer changes.
- **Transparency limits:** Protect competitive methodology, **not truthfulness**: probabilities remain labelled as estimates, precommitted prospective selection results are not invented, data freshness is explicit and no betting profitability or payout verification is claimed. Data provider attribution/commercial licenses are still required where applicable. This cleanup removes current public content; previously committed public Git history may still be accessible and cannot be treated as purged without a separate, controlled migration.
- **Unchanged scope:** Complete premium multi-sport UX, licensing review, authentication/payments, production security and independent prospective accuracy remain open; no checklist gate is prematurely checked.

## 2026-11-03 additional no-credit roadmap work — workflow health, NHL ontology and retired collectors

- **P01-06 / OPS-03 — actual scheduled GitHub workflow health (partial):** Added private `src/nhl-actions-health.cjs`, `scripts/report-nhl-workflow-health.cjs` and deterministic tests. The existing daily no-cost 09:15 UTC audit now checks actual GitHub **scheduled** job conclusions and last-run age independently of successes triggered by code pushes, with explicit `failed`, `overdue`, `cancelled`, `running`, `schedule_evidence_missing` and `job_succeeded` states. The summary/artifact remains private; it does not claim a green workflow proves valid source rows. [Run verified](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41757071184): 5 workflows monitored, one latest scheduled failure (MoneyPuck/DFO); zero overdue and zero critical issues in this observed run. The later MoneyPuck/DFO code-triggered run passed; a passing push is not a passing scheduled collector. No paid API request was introduced. Account-wide quota / alert infrastructure is still incomplete.
- **MLBX-01 / P08-01 — NHL market contract (partial):** Added a private versioned registry of six recognized full-game markets (skater shots on goal, goals, assists, points, blocked shots and goalie saves), explicit market alias matching and goalie-versus-skater role constraints. The main NHL free-source research builder uses the registry to abstain on unsupported/hits/period markets, unavailable directions or conflicting player roles; no market or payout is fabricated. New unit tests and the [NHL suite passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41757395946). Broader multi-sport ontology, original platform rule/settlement verification and market coverage remain open.
- **P01-08 — disable obsolete paid-source workflows (partial):** Kept historical data/code but replaced the deprecated PickFinder TinyFish collector with a zero-request notice, removed PickFinder from the manual NHL category-discovery matrix, and fail-closed the legacy category-batch workflow against injected or retired source inputs. Regression tests prevent accidental reactivation without deliberate tested code changes. Other manual collectors still require separate audit; **no fresh source request or wager** was placed.
- **Private methodology:** The operational audit, NHL market inventory and source provenance remain in the private backend. The customer-facing website retains only supported prop outputs, freshness and verifiable aggregate outcomes; no proprietary model formula or internal lab is reintroduced.
- **Verification and remaining gate:** No new full PrizePicks board capture or explicit prospective NHL graded cohort is implied by these offline reliability changes. Next pregame capture still depends on quota-protected authenticated source availability. The MLB repo remains untouched.

## November 3 NHL original-pregame selection and Nov 2 backtest checkpoint (partial; not prospective)

- **Why the prospective cohort is still empty:** The original November 2 source snapshots recorded offered Higher/Lower sides and uncalibrated model probability vectors, but the policy that picked **one** original direction per individual prop was committed **after** November 2 outcomes. The post-hoc 147-selection reconstruction is not an eligible precommitted result. Recent independent-provider capture attempts returned 503 from zero-credit coverage, no eligible fresh pregame board, or a new cache filename mismatch; no authentic new Git-frozen selection was produced by them. The filename mismatch was found in a safely stopped no-credit run and fixed before any metered API call. The first upcoming pregame cohort still requires an actual successful fresh provider fetch, eligible model-selected directions and a commit strictly before each puck drop.
- **Nov 2 archived data (useful now):** All **four** Nov 2 official NHL games are in the official player-history archive, and the current champion model's player history is updated through Nov 2. Added an independently reproducible [private offline Nov 2 report](outputs/backtests/nhl-2026-11-02-original-line-diagnostic.json), [passing GitHub job](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41760450397), and lookahead/adversarial tests. The *post-outcome reconstructed* original-model policy scored **71/147 (48.3%)** across four games, despite average originally estimated selected-side probability of **66.6%**; always-Lower was **77/147 (52.4%)** on that cohort. This is a potential calibration warning, NOT proven future calibration error.
- **Six independent model methods on the same archived offer lines:** Strictly date-prior NHL player games were used to recalculate six candidate directions on 145 of the 147 archived November 2 original offers (two excluded for unavailable candidate probabilities); replayed original-form benchmark 72/145, 5-game 72/145, 10-game 76/145, prior average 76/145, stable blend 74/145, fixed shrinkage 76/145. This is a **retrospective method comparison** on selected lines; methods and cohorts are correlated, these figures are not model-promoted or verified original selected-line profitability. Production champion unchanged; train/test temporal-split and future untouched cohorts remain enforced.
- **P03-03, P03-04, EDGE-01, OPS-03 partial safeguards:** Versioned original individual-prop policy now undergoes a fail-closed pregame check for an eligible nonempty individual Higher/Lower side, official NHL game ID/start and a safe time margin **before writing a new prospective research snapshot**. A follow-on **post-Git-push verifier** checks exact frozen selections, one immutable snapshot commit, original NHL schedule/player/team alignment and commit timestamp strictly before puck drop. A board by itself no longer disables subsequent guarded trial attempts; the one-shot skip gate demands verified original precommitted selections. Old snapshots remain retrospective. Existing official-NHL-stat grader will accept explicit committed selections only after actual game results, keeping pending and pushes separate. No platform settlement or ROI is certified.
- **Credit and reliability protection:** The independent API trial first validates cached official season schedule, player history and model readiness with no sports-API requests, and refreshes its local NHL projections from already archived history. After a successful quota-guarded full-board capture it **commits source data and pregame projections before attempting model research** so a later downstream failure cannot erase the board. Rebase retries retain immutable per-run files; later commit saves individual selections. Another no-credit scheduled check runs before the 09:25 Pacific November 4–6 one-shot windows. Removed the one-shot capture's push trigger to prevent code edits from spending credits; only its fixed dates or explicit manual dispatch can call it. An accidental workflow retry stopped **before** contacting the paid provider due to the date-filename mismatch; [see safe-run evidence](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41761052748).
- **Not yet achieved:** No new Nov 4 real PrizePicks offer board, zero prospectively *graded* explicitly frozen NHL selections, no validated probability calibration, positive betting expected return, reliable special-tier payouts, authenticated fantasy-platform settlement, or commercial-license review. Do not claim any of those outcomes until independently evidenced. Work is contained to the NHL private backend; public website model IP stays hidden and the historical MLB project remains untouched.

## November 4, 2026 — live board pagination diagnosis and two-day private retrospective audit (partial)

- **Actual 09:25 Pacific source attempt:** [Scheduled prospective freeze run](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41927731484) failed during its **metered** PrizePicksAPI compact pagination. Cached NHL history and model prerequisites passed first (21 eligible upcoming official games, latest stats Nov 3), but the independent provider returned different `meta.observed_at` values across successive paid `/projections` pages. The complete 2026-11-04 board was **not** published. This is a documented non-atomic pagination race, not an acceptable reason to falsely label partial pages as a complete current board. Exact charge and remaining workspace quota from the failed run were **not** archived; do not infer zero spend.
- **No-credit mitigation (P01-06/P03-03 partial):** `src/prizepicksapi-nhl-coverage-stability.cjs` and `preflightNhlBoardStability` now require two consistent **zero-credit `/coverage`** NHL observations 12 seconds apart before paid pagination, validate league status and age, and reject unstable/missing or stale scope. This *reduces*, but cannot eliminate, the chance of refreshing during the following pages. Original same-observation strict pagination remains enforced. If a refresh still happens, write safe **aggregate-only** metadata (page number, returned row counts, charged-credit totals from returned headers, last known quota and observation timestamps) to a private GitHub Actions artifact; never emit API keys, raw offers, partial board or prospective picks. The next scheduled one-shot window remains Nov 5 at 09:25 Pacific subject to source/credit gates; no unscheduled metered request was launched by this change. [Full NHL tests passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41938945741). Provider [documentation](https://prizepicksapi.com/prizepicks-api-pagination) explicitly states live projection pages are evaluated separately and a traversal crossing refresh boundaries must be discarded; the more expensive historical `/board?as_of` reconstruction was **not** silently substituted.
- **Official games and past-only model replay (EDGE-05/P03-04 partial):** November 3 official player-stats archive has **11/11 complete games**. New reproducible **private** [Nov 3 diagnostic](outputs/backtests/nhl-2026-11-03-original-line-diagnostic.json) and [combined two-day summary](outputs/backtests/nhl-nov2-nov3-retrospective-summary.json) audit 201/201 reconstructed Nov 3 individual selection decisions (122 correct, **60.7%**) and the prior Nov 2 cohort (71/147, **48.3%**). Combined 193/348 (**55.5%**) remains **postgame-rule retrospective**, not prospectively frozen, and excludes offer authentication/settlement or verified payout ROI. Individual prop probabilities are **uncalibrated**: the two dates average original estimated selected-side probabilities around 66%; all candidate replays take only prior game logs. A stable-shrink variant on Nov 3 was 121/201 in an exploratory replay. Differences are not independent evidence for model promotion.
- **Security and release status:** No customer-facing model internals or formula details added. No MLB changes or wagers. Latest first-party-proven pregame selected individual cohort remains **zero**. The public research website must continue withholding live picks until source and Git-pregame selections meet the existing gates. Leave broad roadmap checkboxes unchecked until their full acceptance criteria are met.

## November 4 NHL alternate PrizePicks source decision — private The Odds API backup (P01-05 / P03-03 partial)

- **Existing source:** Primary live PrizePicks-labelled rows are supplied by **independent `prizepicksapi.com`**, not directly by PrizePicks. On November 4 [the 09:25 Pacific collector failed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41927731484) after `meta.observed_at` changed during paid pagination. The fail-closed behavior was correct; do not publish inconsistent pages or assert those requests were free.
- **Alternative source comparison:** [The Odds API](https://the-odds-api.com/sports-odds-data/bookmaker-apis.html) advertises bookmaker `prizepicks` in `us_dfs`, including regular player prop markets and Goblin/Demon-indicated alternate lines. [SportsGameOdds](https://sportsgameodds.com/bookmakers/prizepicks-odds-api) also advertises NHL PrizePicks-labelled line coverage, and [OddsPapi](https://oddspapi.io/sportsbooks/prizepicks) exposes the `prizepicks` slug. Provider claims do not prove active availability, accurate original PrizePicks tiers, commercial redistribution rights, exact multipliers or our plan's rights.
- **New backup (partial):** Added `src/nhl-oddsapi-prizepicks-backup.cjs`, `scripts/collect-nhl-prizepicks-oddsapi-backup.cjs`, and a private model/freeze/commit verification path. Only verified official NHL fixtures and unambiguous player identities are accepted; pregame regular full-game NHL markets are mapped into our model, stale/started events rejected and alternate markets preserved **as unverified alternate** (never silently classed as exact Goblin/Demon or paid out). The provider source is tagged correctly and one- or two-game subsets are never treated as complete NHL boards. If an actual model direction is frozen and its Git commit predates puck drop, that cohort may support source-labelled prospective **direction** auditing only; original PrizePicks availability, settlement, authenticated tiers and ROI remain unverified.
- **No-surprise cost controls:** New fallback [workflow](../.github/workflows/nhl-prizepicks-oddsapi-backup.yml) makes no metered calls on code pushes. A bounded one-off schedule for **Nov 5–6 at 09:40 AM Pacific** runs only if the preceding primary attempt did not already yield a Git-verified prospective individual selection cohort; a separate no-cost test workflow runs on changes. The alternate API preflights its shared account balance and official cached schedule with no-cost endpoints, reserves **350 credits**, requests at most **2 NHL events × 8 market keys = 16 reported request credits**, fails closed if cost/identity is unexpected and never auto-retries a paid HTTP failure. This is a deliberate proof-of-coverage trial, **not** guaranteed full-slate fallback or proof that the current account can afford capture.
- **Verification:** The [offline backup integration tests passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41940998550) using mocked vendor responses; **no real alternative PrizePicks board has been captured from this new source yet.** Post-trial determine whether to expand to all eligible NHL games or select SportsGameOdds/OddsPapi, with account-wide spending and commercial rights checked first. Keep full-market backtest/model-promotion and original-platform settlement gates open; do not check larger roadmap tasks complete.

Alternative paid calls are blocked unless the **same UTC calendar day's scheduled primary one-shot** has completed **with failure**; missing, queued, running, cancelled, successful or unknown primary states fail closed. This prevents delayed GitHub cron jobs from accidentally running two provider captures together. The existing Git-verified frozen cohort guard and shared 350-credit Odds API reserve still apply. [Independent offline regression suite](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/41942875773) passed.

A successful fallback source selection frozen before games can establish a prospective **independent-source direction sample**, not authenticated original PrizePicks availability or validated payout economics. No true prospective decided picks or verified edge added, and production formula/MLB remained unchanged. Partial roadmap items remain unchecked.

## November 6 — 06:00 baseline / 09:25 delta-only NHL research design

- **November 6 correction and separation:** Verified production `nhl-bounded-daily-pregame-freeze.yml` invokes `trial-free-prizepicks-nhl.yml` at **09:25 PST**, which still retrieves **a fresh full paginated independent board** and then calculates observed changes. `/api/v1/changes` is an optional incremental event stream, **not** the sole required endpoint for new players or full board completeness; each event may need a `/projections` look-up and periodic full-board reconciliation. Under the currently approved GitHub workflow there is **no active 06:00 source capture**, so the 06:00→09:25 report remains unavailable until a complete same-day 06:00 archive exists. The delta tracker now explicitly searches same-day 06:00 snapshots even when other archive events occur between captures. **Discord is exclusively for the private brigue PrizePicks Following account, not general NHL market changes.** No authorized automatic Following-feed reader is connected, and no real-money entry automation is active.
- **P02-10 / SHARP-02 / SHARP-04 partial:** `src/nhl-morning-source-delta.cjs` now classifies two independently archived complete same-Pacific-date source snapshots (06:00-07:00 PST and 09:25-10:25 PST) into (1) newly observed player-game offers, (2) new stat categories for existing players, (3) new thresholds/tiers for existing markets, (4) newly observed Higher/Lower directions for an existing threshold, (5) changed lines for stable projection identity and (6) old rows missing from the later source **without claiming original-platform removal**. A missing or stale 06:00 baseline fails closed rather than comparing to a different day. Exact provider IDs and source lines remain in the private archive.
- **Existing source workflow integration:** `scripts/track-free-nhl-line-movement.cjs` appends compact `morningDelta` classifications to the existing immutable `movement-latest.json` and movement snapshots already committed by the known PrizePicks source collector, at zero additional API requests by the comparison logic. To detect changes, an actual second source observation is still needed. The 09:25 source workflow currently retains its original full-board capture and model-freeze behavior, rather than making an untested claim that it consumes only deltas.
- **Schedule status — BLOCKED / NOT DEPLOYED:** The 06:00 capture was requested and the two-slot quota gate was developed in an *unmerged* draft branch, but the GitHub workflow schedule edit was blocked by tooling safeguards. The effective production schedule therefore remains **09:25 PST Nov 6–13** (one bounded scheduled source attempt daily). Do not represent the 06:00 baseline or a paired morning report as live until the workflow has actually been authorized, merged and run. Provider billing still requires request-meter verification, 100 legacy requests reserve and bounded page limit. The independent provider's `/changes` endpoint exists, but is **not** invoked or billed by this change.
- **CI corrective fix:** `package.json` now scopes `npm test` to `test/*.cjs`; previously a one-time Discord-connectivity script whose basename started with `test-` was discovered by Node's automatic test runner and failed if a webhook secret was unavailable. The corrected unit-suite still exercises the complete `test/` directory and does not accidentally send a live Discord message.
- **brigue account scope:** A verified automatic PrizePicks social-following feed or authorized auto-entry integration has not been established. The third-party public **prop** change feed contains no brigue lineup authorship. Private Discord webhook sending works, but no automatic brigue feed monitoring/entry is active. A permitted signed social source or direct brigue-posted public channel is required.
- **Verification:** [GitHub feature-branch NHL tests passed](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/actions/runs/42205734487). Full broad roadmap acceptance tasks remain unchecked. No paid feed queries, external wagers or personal account access in this work.

## Project completion definition

A professional system is ready when the website offers:

- automated source-compatible PrizePicks and Underdog quotes, clearly labelled freshness and rights;
- reproducible sport-specific statistically validated projections;
- trustworthy **prospective** probability calibration, transparent risk and quality;
- verified applicable payout/rules for any EV claims;
- correlation-aware, rules-compliant, verified 2–6 pick research lineup construction;
- season and per-market track records separated by platform and settlement type;
- reliable mobile performance and monitoring, with spend controls and no wagers placed automatically.

**Current verdict:** NHL data engineering and prototype UI are meaningfully underway. The main barrier to a trustworthy **commercial subscription** is prospective validated model accuracy, precise payout/settlement evidence, full platform comparison, source licensing, payments/legal approval, member security and an integrated mobile website—not merely acquiring more historical quotes. **MLB is explicitly last.**

## VERIFIED November 8, 2026 — 2025-selected models, 2026 retrospective check

[PR #76 merged](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform/pull/76). Relative to last-5-game averages in the **retrospective 2026** cohort, **16 of 17** development-year-chosen models had lower MAE and one tied (receiving touchdowns). Selected 2025 strategies included career averages for passing yards, receiving yards, FG made and kicking points; shrunk last-5 for rushing yards and receptions; EWMA(0.35) for rushing attempts. Example 2026 differences: passing yards chosen career MAE 67.5322 vs last-5 71.3171 (5.31% lower), kicking points 2.9946 vs 3.1778 (5.76% lower), rushing yards shrunk last-5 20.6168 vs 21.2095 (2.79% lower), receiving yards career 22.2974 vs 22.5133 (0.96% lower).

**Limitations unchanged:** The selection algorithm was *designed after 2026 data existed*; this is not blinded prospective model selection. Outcomes are conditional on recorded game rows/attempting kickers, not a full pregame population. None of the 17 results are offered-line Higher/Lower hit rates or expected return, and they cannot justify model promotion or automatic picks. Source licensing, original platform historical quotes and settlements, and authentic future-frozen forecasts are still pending.

## November 10, 2026 — NFL original-Standard Higher miss audit and future-only shadow abstention study

**Original evidence / not hindsight picks:** November 9 original [independently frozen NFL snapshot](../outputs/nfl/prospective/20261109T172651Z.json) contains **115 Standard side-qualified research selections**, all saved in the private first Git commit before events. The [strict official-derived outcome grade](../outputs/nfl/backtests/pregame-forecast-proof-latest.json) currently has **29 decided**, **15 correct**, **14 incorrect**, **1 push**, **85 pending** (83 upcoming Nov 12/15 and two unresolved player appearances); all 29 decided were in **one BUF vs MIN game**. Original side choices are never changed or backdated. **No original first-party fantasy-app quote, regional lineup eligibility, platform settlement or payout is confirmed.**

**Cause diagnosis — audited descriptively, not causal proof:** Across 29 first-Git model directions, **18 chose Higher** (5 hit, **13 missed because actual Lower**), **11 chose Lower** (10 hit, one missed because actual Higher). **24 of 29 individual point projections exceeded their eventual player statistic**, though averaging raw forecast errors *across different market units* is invalid. Relative to the same original selection thresholds, hypothetical always-Lower was 23/29 vs model 15/29, but it is NOT a full-board executable betting strategy. Team split: all 5 Minnesota Higher selections missed, versus 8 of 13 Bills Higher selections; all four rushing-yards Higher selections lost. The Bills won 23–6, controlling possession 42:32 vs 17:28; Minnesota had 46 offensive plays to Buffalo's 79, an unusually asymmetric usage environment. See [team recap](https://www.buffalobills.com/news/bills-23-vikings-6-final-score-recap-highlights) and [NFL original final stats](https://static.www.nfl.com/image/upload/v1794285215/gamecenter/ab5825a7-4feb-11f1-abca-2c54536568a9.pdf). The current projection is a past-only, last-five/career-shrink boxscore estimate; it does not verify pregame opponent-specific pace, injury/starting role or shifting usage. **Game-script dependence, incomplete role/usage and one-sided quoted-market sample selection are hypotheses**, not isolated causal estimates. One finished NFL game does not prove a league-wide bias or an always-Lower edge.

**Delivered code and private source integrity:** `src/nfl-frozen-direction-diagnostic.cjs` + `scripts/report-nfl-frozen-direction-diagnostic.cjs` strictly rejoin every graded outcome to its **original immutable first Git** frozen market, line, offered/selected side, historical projection, game identity and 15-minute kickoff threshold; reject duplicates and conflicting/altered outcomes. Report per-market projection-minus-actual bias (never combine yards with targets/receptions), team splits, small projected-margin Higher misses and original sample overlap; private report `outputs/nfl/backtests/frozen-direction-bias-diagnostic-latest.json`. Test suite includes explicit fake-result/modified Git rejection. [Public free-runner private full regression and real diagnosis](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/42795394663) **passed**.

**New prospective challengers — PARTIAL pending new original board:** `src/nfl-prospective-shadow.cjs` preregisters two symmetric *research abstention only* rules: (a) require an absolute original past-only forecast margin of at least **10% of original line**; (b) same margin **and** agreement between the original last-five average direction and the original shrunk forecast direction. These thresholds were defined **after the November 9 game outcome was seen**. They are expressly exploratory hypotheses; they cannot be applied retrospectively to that game as if frozen then. Policies **never flip original sides**, never infer availability or a model-derived probability, and never affect production picks. `scripts/freeze-nfl-prospective-shadows.cjs` only uses source-verified **newly observed** original NFL snapshots no more than 30 minutes old and still >=15 minutes before each kickoff; each shadow requires its *own independent remote first-Git original commit* before it is counted. `src/nfl-shadow-postgame-grade.cjs` + `scripts/report-nfl-shadow-postgame.cjs` grade future eligible observations on the same original threshold, exclude duplicate repeated 6/9 snapshots and keep exact same-cohort hypothetical Higher/Lower comparisons. No previous game or numeric-only research forecast is silently promoted to prospective evidence.

**Operational follow-through required:** After merging, run public free-runner private grading/report archive and attach a public-runner NFL **future original snapshot → new original-Git shadow precommit** workflow. New claims remain `PARTIAL` until independent remote Git evidence, source permissions, subsequent final results, market-specific sample size and game-cluster uncertainty have all been checked. No payout EV, calibrated percentages, automatically trusted slip or wager placement. Continue collecting unchanged NHL/NFL 6 and 9 AM snapshots, and never claim a missed capture was reconstructed.


## November 10, 2026 — NFL four-context-factor model + agreed research websites: implementation and strict gates

**User request:** build opponent defense, own offensive performance and pace, player usage and injury/role context, and game weather; also incorporate our already-selected research websites **without falsely claiming they supply a licensed live feed**. Work delivered in private `feat/nfl-pregame-matchup-context-all-sources-20261110`, public free-runner validation and future private source workflow. [Source-by-source detailed design](NFL_PREGAME_MATCHUP_INTEGRATION_2026-11-10.md).

**Implemented/tested (bounded code milestones):**
- New `src/nfl-pregame-matchup-challenger.cjs` uses actual **nflverse historical team_weekly + player_weekly + snap_counts**, including opponent passing/rushing allowed (prior weeks only), own offense/team pace, and strictly lagged player target/carry/pass volume and snap share. Distinct numerical *shadow* projections change with those features. No post-game or target-week data, no original selection/posted-line rewrite.
- **Weather**: separate exact venue/game/issued/observed time guard accepts **only an actual pregame Open-Meteo-style forecast run**, never historical actual weather, and computes a strictly experimental weather adjustment only when real evidence exists. **Injuries**: exact timestamped player-game injury `QUESTIONABLE/DOUBTFUL/OUT` causes research abstention; missing statuses are unknown. At present these live sources have **no verified authorized active collector**, so missing entries stay missing.
- **Next Gen and PFR**: prior-week exact-ID advanced covariates recognized and tracked; coefficients not deceptively added without feature validity. **FTN charting**: existing two-week-embargo, CC BY-SA 4.0 retrospective *actual numeric* challenger remains in the archive with prior-game context; it worsened all eight evaluated 2026 market MAEs when added naively, and is not promoted to closed production.
- **RotoWire, Fantasy Life, PFN and PFF**: explicitly registry-listed and license-gated; PFF newly added to NFL family market feature matrices. Premium consumer accounts and publicly reachable webpages are **not** considered authorized automated/commercial API data. Import of separately permitted pregame snapshots requires source/time/game/identity match **and independent original Git proof**; none of these paid feeds is presently verified as connected.
- Future precommit: `scripts/build-nfl-matchup-shadow.cjs` + public `private-nfl-matchup-context-future-shadow.yml` create a distinct **first-Git pregame** private context research record from the same freshly frozen NFL Standard original offers, without touching the champion or changing already frozen directions. Missing/stale source produces an explicit skip.
- Future grading: `src/nfl-matchup-shadow-grading.cjs` + `scripts/report-nfl-matchup-shadow.cjs` compare model vs unchanged original **on exactly matched first-Git lines** after games complete, with pushes, duplicate offers, pending games and separate market units handled honestly. Existing public-runner NFL final-stat grader will update and privately commit the forward-only report.
- Existing NHL **MoneyPuck/Daily Faceoff** research integrations preserved. **Evolving-Hockey/HockeyViz** remain separate NHL research references. No MLB code or historical NHL snapshots changed.

**Testing and preexisting results:** Historical 2026 context evaluation on **3,924 observed player-market rows in 6 weeks** found small opponent/pace benefit in 6/8 categories, worse **receiving yards**, and naive FTN charting contribution worse in all 8; **not blind, no authenticated posted-line EV**. [Final public free-runner private branch regression and historical replay](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/workflows/test-private-nfl-all-matchup-sources.yml) validates source-timing traps, weather/injury licensing, exact-stat performance change, full NHL/NFL suite and Ftn license gate. **No original first-Git-context future cohort graded yet.**

**Open/blocked roadmap acceptance:** validated pregame injury and venue coordinates/weather collectors, licensed premium PFF/RotoWire/FantasyLife/PFN feeds and current roster/route data, position-specific pressure/coverage features, logged point-in-time producer versions, independent forward-heldout win rates across multiple games and platform quotes/settlements/payout EV. **Do not promote the challenger, publish prediction probabilities or auto-construct wagering lineups** until these pass. The first actual next NFL future kickoff/archival result must be independently verified; historical model MAE is not first-Git live-prop hit rate.


## November 10, 2026 — Daily pregame NHL + NFL board movement, including non-game days

**Why:** NFL has just one Week 10 Thursday game (Nov 12), twelve Sunday games (Nov 15), and one Monday game (Nov 16). The research website should record available full-board prices and lineup-side eligibility **every morning, even on Tuesday/Wednesday/Friday/Saturday**, because original odds, lines, tiers, participation, and offered side may change days before kickoff. NHL remains collected on the same two daily slots, not dropped in favor of NFL.

**Existing verified infrastructure:** The **public** GitHub Actions `.github/workflows/public-private-nhl-nfl-0900.yml` already schedules both NHL and NFL full-source collections at 06:00 and 09:00 Pacific, with retries at :12/:27/:43/:51 inside each hour, and stores original offer IDs, source timestamps, lines and side indicators in **private GitHub**. The honest Nov 10 06:20 Pacific archive captured **1,859 NHL and 2,997 NFL source offers**, both source-complete, with separately verified Nov 10 06:24 original first-Git frozen 213 NHL selections (52 Standard) and 192 NFL Standard selections. Public `data/nhl-nfl-freeze-status.json` and `data/collection-health.json` confirm that these are genuine pregame observations, not authenticated regional app orders or verified expected value. Missing Nov 10 9 AM should remain explicitly missing until it actually runs successfully.

**New implementation:** Updated `scripts/diff-live-partner-captures.cjs` to discover the most recent independently verified complete NHL+NFL morning archive **across up to seven preceding days**. When the current capture is the same day's 9 AM, prefer the real same-day 6 AM archive; only that pairing may be called a genuine 06→09 comparison. When the current capture is 6 AM, compare it with yesterday's most recent complete morning source (or the most recent available prior day) and label it **cross-day**. Preserve per-sport original offer IDs and counts for changed lines, newly observed IDs, missing-source IDs (not confirmed platform removals), tier changes, offered-side changes, kickoff changes, and unchanged IDs. Archive private reports in `outputs/partner-source/line-delta/YYYY-MM-DD/`. Refuse falsified capture-hour, manual, low-inventory, incomplete or stale source manifests rather than claiming successful comparisons. Frozen pregame model choices are immutable; a moved later line is not substituted into an older frozen prediction.

**Verified testing:** [Public free-runner branch test for daily NHL/NFL source differences](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/42837926574) passed synthetic cross-day/same-day/fake source tests, full NHL/NFL/MLB private Node regression, and a real prior-archive lookup of Nov 10 6 AM against Nov 9 9 AM. The deployment workflow is next required to validate the merged main and write/commit actual cross-day reports; a green test does not prove every future schedule succeeds.

**Remaining gates:** monitor genuine automatic 06/09 captures for several consecutive days; backfill independently derived **comparison reports only** from real archives, never manufacture historical 6 AM observations; extend safe count-only movement dashboard and eventual approved Underdog comparisons; keep paid website feeds/settlements/calibration explicitly unavailable until verified. No wagers placed.


### Nov 10 daily comparison integrity correction — historic wrong flag, exclude manual sources

The first cross-day reconciliation inadvertently selected a real **Nov 9 8:01 PM Pacific manual board** as the baseline because `verifiedSlot` also permits legitimate manually captured archives. Its line-change counts were valid across *two real observations* but **did not measure the planned morning-to-morning comparison**. Both raw source boards and the Nov 10 original pregame frozen selections remained immutable.

The true Nov 9 **09:20–09:22 AM Pacific** source is archived at `outputs/manual-board-captures/2026-11-09/20261109T172217Z` and has both complete NHL (927) and NFL (1,808) source inventories, original time in the 09 hour and `actualCaptureInRequestedHour=true`. A known legacy collector bug had incorrectly set `notSixOrNineAmSlot=true` in that immutable manifest. The corrected comparison script independently verifies the actual original source start/end Pacific hours, day, complete dual-sport inventories and slots. It accepts this **specific dated legacy exception** while rejecting all manual out-of-hour sources; it does **not** alter the historical source manifest.

The initial **derived comparison report** is corrected in Git with explicit `correction` provenance, so its prior version remains in private Git history. Public published counts must be republished from the corrected morning baseline, not the 8 PM manual board. First-Git selections never change. [Verified full regression and real prior morning source](https://github.com/Thedarkwillow/nhl-betting-platform1/actions/runs/42839607115) passed. Keep this distinction in all public summary headlines and do not call a cross-day report the same-day 06→09 line movement.
